Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). _source line 70 · 2026-05-15T19:39:38.552Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The resource usage tool shows only 2 experiments as "actually running" (cuda:0 and cuda:1), but from the reconcile_crons output, there are more running experiments including:
- exp_004_sim_sweep_extreme_gamma (pid=528993) - CPU
- exp_001_sft_d025_3B_v3 (pid=589763) - cuda:5
- exp_001_sft_3B_d100_s123_a32_v2 (pid=594489) - cuda:0


- exp_001_sft_3B_d100_s42_a32_v2 (pid=593740) - cuda:1
- exp_001_sft_3B_d100_s42_a32 (pid=593745) - likely on another GPU

Looking at the roadmap, I can see what should be running on each GPU: cuda:2 has the 3B d005 s123 aligned eval, cuda:3 has the 3B d100 s123 para_aligned eval, cuda:4 is running the 3B d000 v3 SFT plus d025_s42 signals, and cuda:5 has the 3B d025 v3 SFT. The resource usage tool appears to be pulling data from the registry, which might explain why it's not capturing the full picture of what's actually running.

The critical update is that exp_001_sft_3B_d000_v3 wasn't actually failed—it was a false alarm from the cron tracking the wrong PID. I've restored it to RUNNING with the correct PID 597179 and re-registered the cron job. The training is progressing at about 56% (step 1746 out of 3125), and since it's using epoch-based checkpointing, the first save won't happen until the end of epoch 1. This means D068's decision to hold off on resubmitting d000_v3 was the right call for resource management, even though the underlying assumption about it being failed was incorrect.

I'm also flagging this as a recurring infrastructure problem—the submit script is capturing the bash shell PID instead of the actual Python process PID, which needs to be fixed. I'll document this pattern for future reference before updating the Director.
```

7	Cron auto-marked exp_001_eval_3B_d000_v2_s123_para_aligned as failed because: process dead + no sentinel file + no W&B run. But the experiment actually completed successfully with all output files present.
8	
9	**Why:** Eval experiments don't always produce sentinel files or W&B runs. Cron's "process dead 5x" heuristic doesn't check for output completeness.
10	
11	**How to apply:** When cron reports a failed eval experiment, always verify by checking if output files (eval_results.json, correctness_matrix.json, item_responses.json) exist before accepting the failed status. Spawn a diagnostic Worker if needed.
12

  "new_string": "Two known false-positive patterns:\n\n1. **Eval output complete but no sentinel**: Cron auto-marked exp_001_eval_3B_d000_v2_s123_para_aligned as failed (process dead + no sentinel + no W&B). Output files were actually complete.\n\n2. **Wrong PID tracked**: Cron tracked bash shell PID (597174) from submit_training_job instead of actual python process PID (597179). Shell exits immediately after spawning timeout wrapper → cron sees \"dead 5x\" → false failed. Actual training was at 56% on GPU4. (exp_001_sft_3B_d000_v3, 2026-05-15)\n\n**Why:** submit_training_job records the launcher shell PID, not the training process PID. Cron's heuristic doesn't verify GPU utilization or child processes.\n\n**How to apply:** When cron reports failed, ALWAYS spawn a diagnostic Worker to verify with `ps aux | grep python` + `nvidia-smi` before accepting. Never trust PID-only death detection. For training jobs, check if the actual python process and GPU memory usage are still active."
}

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Generate dose-response curves: dosage vs accuracy for ARC/GSM8K/MMLU at 0.5B/1.5B."""
3	
4	import matplotlib
5	matplotlib.use('Agg')
6	import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
7	import matplotlib.patches as mpatches
8	import numpy as np
9	from pathlib import Path
10	
11	plt.rcParams.update({
12	    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',
13	    'font.size': 11,
14	    'axes.titlesize': 13,
15	    'axes.labelsize': 12,
16	    'xtick.labelsize': 10,
17	    'ytick.labelsize': 10,
18	    'legend.fontsize': 10,
19	    'figure.dpi': 300,
20	    'savefig.dpi': 300,
21	    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',
22	    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,
23	    'axes.spines.top': False,
24	    'axes.spines.right': False,
25	    'lines.linewidth': 1.8,
26	})
27	
28	# --- Data from exp-001 registry (aligned accuracy, seed s123) ---
29	xpos = np.arange(4)  # evenly spaced positions
30	dosage_labels = ['0%', '0.5%', '2.5%', '10%']
31	
32	data = {
33	    '0.5B': {
34	        'ARC':   [0.4974, 0.2978, 0.4061, 0.3669],
35	        'GSM8K': [0.3412, 0.3677, 0.3533, 0.3798],
36	        'MMLU':  [0.4216, 0.3002, 0.3473, 0.3058],
37	    },
38	    '1.5B': {
39	        'ARC':   [0.7577, 0.7568, 0.7577, 0.7816],
40	        'GSM8K': [0.6535, 0.6482, 0.6550, 0.6444],
41	        'MMLU':  [0.5855, 0.5869, 0.5881, 0.5956],
42	    },
43	}
44	
45	colors = {
46	    '0.5B': '#E69F00',
47	    '1.5B': '#0072B2',
48	    '3B':   '#999999',
49	}
50	markers = {'0.5B': 'o', '1.5B': 's'}
51	benchmarks = ['ARC', 'GSM8K', 'MMLU']
52	
53	fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(10, 4.2), sharey=False)
54	
55	for idx, (ax, bench) in enumerate(zip(axes, benchmarks)):
56	    for scale in ['0.5B', '1.5B']:
57	        vals = data[scale][bench]
58	        ax.plot(xpos, vals, color=colors[scale], marker=markers[scale],
59	                markersize=6, label=scale, zorder=3)
60	        ax.axhline(y=vals[0], color=colors[scale], linestyle='--',
61	                   linewidth=0.9, alpha=0.35, zorder=1)
62	
63	    # 3B pending placeholder
64	    y_15b_max = max(data['1.5B'][bench])
65	    y_15b_min = min(data['1.5B'][bench])
66	    y_pending = y_15b_max + (y_15b_max - y_15b_min) * 0.3 + 0.02
67	    rect = mpatches.FancyBboxPatch(
68	        (0.3, y_pending - 0.015), 2.4, 0.03,
69	        boxstyle="round,pad=0.008", linewidth=1, edgecolor='#999999',
70	        facecolor='#f5f5f5', linestyle='--', alpha=0.6, zorder=0)
71	    ax.add_patch(rect)
72	    ax.text(1.5, y_pending, '[PENDING-3B]', ha='center', va='center',
73	            fontsize=7.5, color='#999999', style='italic')
74	    if idx == 0:
75	        ax.plot([], [], color=colors['3B'], linestyle='--', linewidth=1.2, label='3B [PENDING]')
76	
77	    ax.set_title(bench, fontweight='bold')
78	    ax.set_xticks(xpos)
79	    ax.set_xticklabels(dosage_labels)
80	    ax.set_xlabel('Contamination Dosage')
81	    if idx == 0:
82	        ax.set_ylabel('Accuracy')
83	
84	    ax.set_xlim(-0.4, 3.4)
85	    ymin_all = min(min(data['0.5B'][bench]), min(data['1.5B'][bench]))
86	    ymax_all = max(y_pending + 0.03, max(data['1.5B'][bench]) + 0.03)
87	    ax.set_ylim(ymin_all - 0.04, ymax_all + 0.02)
88	
89	# --- Annotations ---
90	
91	# ARC: forgetting arrow on 0.5B drop d000→d005
92	ax_arc = axes[0]
93	arc_05 = data['0.5B']['ARC']

97	    fontsize=8.5, color=colors['0.5B'],
98	    arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='->', color=colors['0.5B'], lw=1.2),
99	    ha='center', va='bottom')
100	
101	# GSM8K: inflation arrow on 0.5B upward trend
102	ax_gsm = axes[1]
103	gsm_05 = data['0.5B']['GSM8K']
104	ax_gsm.annotate(
105	    'Inflation ($\\gamma\\!>\\!0$)',
106	    xy=(3, gsm_05[3]), xytext=(1.8, gsm_05[3] + 0.045),
107	    fontsize=8.5, color=colors['0.5B'],
108	    arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='->', color=colors['0.5B'], lw=1.2),
109	    ha='center', va='bottom')
110	
111	# MMLU: position bias shading for 0.5B region
112	ax_mmlu = axes[2]
113	mmlu_05 = data['0.5B']['MMLU']
114	y_lo = min(mmlu_05) - 0.015
115	y_hi = max(mmlu_05) + 0.015
116	ax_mmlu.axhspan(y_lo, y_hi, color='#ffcccc', alpha=0.3, zorder=0)
117	ax_mmlu.text(1.5, y_hi + 0.006, '⚠ Position bias\n(0.5B MC benchmarks)',
118	             fontsize=6.5, color='#cc3333', ha='center', va='bottom', style='italic')
119	
120	# Single shared legend
121	handles, labels = axes[0].get_legend_handles_labels()
122	fig.legend(handles, labels, loc='upper center', ncol=3,
123	           bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 1.01), frameon=False)
124	
125	plt.tight_layout(rect=[0, 0, 1, 0.93])
126	
127	out_dir = Path(__file__).parent
128	fig.savefig(out_dir / 'fig_dose_response.pdf')
129	fig.savefig(out_dir / 'fig_dose_response.png')
130	print(f"Saved: {out_dir / 'fig_dose_response.pdf'}")
131	print(f"Saved: {out_dir / 'fig_dose_response.png'}")
132	plt.close()

The GPU check ran about 20 minutes after the diagnosis, and with the training at 56% completion with hours remaining, d000_v3 shouldn't have finished—but cuda:4 shows zero memory usage, so it likely crashed in that window. The cron monitor should catch this on the next poll, but for now I'm prioritizing flagging the freed GPUs (cuda:2 and cuda:4) as available for the critical path signals experiment, while noting the d000_v3 failure for investigation.
